Each SPEC borrows only a functional capability description and re-authors it entirely from moai's own SPEC prose — no third-party wording, section structure, code, or file layout was consulted, and the shipped files carry zero vendor attribution (mechanically verified: `grep -ril 'skillstead'` → 0 across all three skill directories, no `NOTICE` file, and the house-convention `license: Apache-2.0` field is either newly added — the two new-skill SPECs — or preserved byte-unchanged — the enhancement SPEC). **(1) `moai-workflow-docs-claim-check`** (NEW skill) — a read-only check of whether a public-facing document's claims (README, release notes, install/usage guides) are supported by user-supplied evidence, running Preflight → Claim Triage → Validation, splitting composite claims into atomic ones and labeling each; it runs no commands and writes no fixes. **(2) `moai-domain-svg-infographic`** (NEW skill) — authors editable SVG technical infographics (architecture / flow / comparison / hierarchy) by computing the layout numerically before writing markup, then renders a 2x PNG via headless Chromium; ships a CJK-first font stack, a deterministic source lint (`scripts/check-svg.mjs`), a render script (`scripts/render.mjs`), and explicit mermaid-vs-SVG selection rules — positioned as additive/complementary to the existing mermaid pipeline, never a replacement. **(3) `moai-domain-humanize` v1.2.0 → v1.3.0** (enhancement) — grafts an Invariant Ledger (pre-edit boundary: a 4-category taxonomy of facts/figures/comparisons/uncertainty, each item marked supplied or inferred, unmarked defaulting to supplied as a fail-safe) and a Delta Audit (post-edit verification across claim-and-intent parity, survival check, and audience/tone/purpose fit) into the skill's existing workflow (steps 2 and 6), strictly additive — the pre-existing S1/S2/S3 severity model, A/B/C/D grading, 30%/50% over-editing guardrails, and 4-language routing are unchanged (regression-verified). All three skills are registered in `internal/template/catalog.yaml` (40 total skills) with byte-identical template↔local mirrors (`make build` clean); `internal/template` neutrality/leak-guard tests pass (0 internal SPEC-ID/REQ/AC tokens, 0 vendor tokens in any shipped file); full `go test ./...` exits 0. PR-mandatory regime (branch `feat/skillport-epic`); single sync commit carries the merged `in-progress → implemented → completed` 3-phase close for all three SPECs. 🗿 MoAI

- **[SPEC-SEC-DEEPSCAN-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-SEC-DEEPSCAN-001/spec.md) + [SPEC-SEC-GUARDIAN-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-SEC-GUARDIAN-001/spec.md)** — Epic SECURITY-ABSORB: two complementary, natively-reimplemented security layers absorbed from the Anthropic official security plugin architecture (Tier L, both PASS — 27/27 and 25/25 AC). **(1) On-demand deep scan** (`/moai review --deep`, SPEC-1) — a heavy, explicit multi-agent vulnerability scan built on Dynamic Workflows: a 6-phase read-only recon → hunt → verify pipeline runs a 3-voter adversarial verification panel (REACHABILITY / IMPACT / DEFENSES, 2-of-3 quorum) over each candidate finding, generates fix patches in an isolated scratch clone (never applies them directly — one finding = one patch = one PR), and writes timestamped JSONL results under `.moai/reports/security-deepscan/` (gitignored). Degrades gracefully on runtimes below Dynamic-Workflows availability (`v2.1.154+`). No shipped script, no new agent — a markdown playbook addition to the existing `review` workflow skill plus a command `argument-hint` update (`--deep`/`--patch`/`--commit <SHA>`). **(2) Always-on in-session guardian** (SPEC-2) — a light, 3-layer inline guardian complementing the on-demand scan: **Layer 1** (`internal/hook/security/`, new `patterns.go`/`scan.go`) is a PostToolUse regex pattern engine (~28 patterns across 10 vulnerability classes, 16-language-neutral) that surfaces advisory warnings on Write/Edit, never blocking; **Layer 2** (`diff.go`) is a Stop-hook turn-end diff review that scans only the turn's added lines and surfaces high-severity findings in the stop message; **Layer 3** (`guardian.go`, dormant by default, opt-in) is a commit-time cross-file review reading changed-plus-related files for cross-file vulnerability classes (e.g. IDOR). All three layers are advisory-first (fail-open on non-git directories, empty stdin, or malformed JSON) with blocking gated behind an explicit `MOAI_SECURITY_BLOCKING` opt-in and a `--skip-hook` audit trail. Shipped via three Template-First shell wrappers (`handle-security-{scan,turn,commit}.sh`) + `moai hook security-{scan,turn,commit}` Go subcommands + `settings.json` wiring (Layer 1 async); `internal/hook/security` coverage 90.0%. Both SPECs preserve the strict subagent-user-interaction boundary (0 `AskUserQuestion`/`mcp__askuser` references in any hook or scanner path). Full `go test ./...` + cross-platform (`GOOS=windows`) builds exit 0; `golangci-lint` 0 new issues. 🗿 MoAI

### Fixed

- **[SPEC-PRETOOL-GATE-MOVE-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-PRETOOL-GATE-MOVE-001/spec.md)** — relocated the heavy commit-quality gate (vet/lint/test) off the PreToolUse 5s hook budget to a native git pre-commit hook, closing census C-2 (CRITICAL): the gate's deny verdict was silently dropped after 5s, and under maintainer `defaultMode: bypassPermissions` that PreToolUse deny was the sole blocking mechanism between Claude Code and a bad commit (Tier M, 15/15 AC PASS). **(1) `moai gate` verb** (`internal/cli/gate.go`, NEW) — thin cobra wrapper exposing `QualityGate.Run` as a standalone CLI command, invoked by the pre-commit hook so the full step budget (vet 30s + lint 60s + test 120s per `gate.yaml`) is available outside Claude Code's 5s PreToolUse budget. **(2) `preCommitHookContent` extension** (`internal/cli/hook_install_precommit.go`) — the SPEC-PRECOMMIT-001 installer's hook body now invokes `moai gate` after the existing fast subset (`gofmt -l` + `go vet`), guarded by `command -v moai` (16-language neutrality: non-moai projects pass silently) and `SKIP_MOAI_PRECOMMIT=1` (bypass parity with `SKIP_MOAI_PREPUSH`); byte-identical mirror shipped at `internal/template/templates/.git_hooks/pre-commit` (closing a pre-existing gap where the template file was absent). **(3) `--no-verify` bypass defense** (`internal/hook/pre_tool.go`, F5 mechanical) — a fast sub-millisecond regex guard denies `git commit --no-verify` (standalone or compound) via the existing PreToolUse path, because under `bypassPermissions` the PreToolUse deny is the sole blocking mechanism and `--no-verify` would otherwise bypass the relocated gate; `.claude/rules/moai/development/coding-standards.md` §Bash Risk-Amplifier Doctrine names `git commit --no-verify` as a destructive primitive. AC-PGM-003 proves budget independence at the integration boundary (`TestPreCommitRelocation_BudgetIndependence`, 9.91s > 5s); AC-PGM-004 proves the mechanical guard (4 subtests + negative case). Full `go test ./...` exit 0; `golangci-lint` 0 issues; cross-platform (`GOOS=windows GOARCH=amd64`) build exit 0; subagent boundary grep 0; template neutrality 0 (no SPEC/REQ tokens, `#!/bin/sh` POSIX, no macOS-bias paths). PRESERVE verified: ast-grep scanner (SPEC-FALSE-ALLCLEAR-GUARD-001), `IsGitCommit`/`detectToolchain`/16-language table, `PrePushInstaller`, frozen-zone guard all untouched. **Known debt (transparent)**: package-aggregate coverage `internal/cli` 75.2% / `internal/hook` 83.4% (acceptance.md §F target ≥85%) — touched files are well-covered, the package aggregate lags on legacy code; pre-existing ast-grep findings (`pkg/version/version.go`, `test/integration/harness/it02_tier3_test.go`) now surface via `moai gate` exit 1 rather than being silently dropped (the documented `SKIP_MOAI_PRECOMMIT=1` escape hatch applies). Route B (PR-mandatory, this repo's branch-protection override); single sync commit carries the merged `in-progress → implemented → completed` 3-phase close. 🗿 MoAI

- **[SPEC-FALSE-ALLCLEAR-GUARD-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-FALSE-ALLCLEAR-GUARD-001/spec.md)** — closed two false all-clear defects found by a codebase census: unconditional `slog` suppression across every CLI subcommand, and `moai ast-grep` reporting a clean scan when the `sg` scanner binary was absent (Tier M, 2 milestones, 18/18 AC PASS; independent sync-audit CONDITIONAL PASS 89.9/100, both must-pass dimensions — Functionality 90, Security 95 — clearing independently). **(1) M1 — logging scope** (`internal/cli/logging.go`, new) — `InitDependencies`/`initLightDeps` had each installed a discarding `slog` handler unconditionally for every subcommand, silencing all 218 warn-and-above call sites (198 `Warn` + 20 `Error`) and leaving `MOAI_LOG_LEVEL` wired to config but reaching no handler. Logging is now decided once, in `Execute()`, before the trivial/full-init branch: `moai hook <event>` keeps the discarding handler (stdout carries the hook's JSON contract, stderr belongs to the Claude Code runtime), every other subcommand writes warn-and-above to stderr, and `MOAI_LOG_LEVEL` (read via the existing `config.EnvLogLevel` constant) raises or lowers that bar, falling back to `warn` on an unparseable value. `InitDependencies` now inherits the process default instead of re-installing one (measured `slog.SetDefault` site count 2 → 1). **(2) M2 — ast-grep scanner sentinel** (`internal/astgrep/scanner.go`) — `Scanner.Scan` returned `([]Finding{}, nil)` when `sg` could not be resolved, indistinguishable from a clean scan; it now returns `ErrScannerUnavailable`, wrapped so the message names the binary and carries install guidance while `errors.Is` still matches through the wrap (`ValidateBinary` still runs first, so a rejected binary path stays a distinct, non-sentinel error). `moai ast-grep` writes the guidance to stderr and exits non-zero (previously exit 0 + `no findings` + 0 stderr bytes) — a breaking change for any scripted caller relying on the old exit-0 behavior; `moai ast-edit` deliberately keeps exit 0 (a mutator's "nothing to apply" is a genuine no-op). The commit-time PreToolUse quality gate still never blocks on `sg` being absent, but the pass now carries a reason — two named constants distinguish "scanner absent" from "scan failed", propagated through `QualityGate.Run` and emitted on `HookOutput.SystemMessage` (not `slog`, since the `moai hook` path discards records under M1). `moai doctor` gained an `sg` check (warn-not-fail, mirroring the existing `checkGitHubCLI` version-override seam so golden snapshots stay host-independent), and all four docs-site locales were corrected — each previously claimed both commands exit without an error, which is now false for `ast-grep`. Six pre-existing tests that asserted the silent pass were inverted (not dropped) so they fail again if the false all-clear returns; none was weakened. Verified: 18/18 AC PASS; `go test -count=1 ./...` exit 0 (105 packages, 0 FAIL); both `AC-FAG-011` falsification forms (call-deleted in `pre_tool.go`, body-neutered in `astgrep_gate.go`) fail in detached probe worktrees, plus a third auditor-added form; `go vet`, Windows and Linux cross-builds exit 0; scoped `gofmt` clean (the 106 pre-existing repo-wide unclean files are untouched, explicitly out of scope); coverage improved or held flat across all five touched packages (`internal/astgrep` 88.7→89.0, `internal/hook/quality` 87.5→87.6, `internal/hook` 83.3→83.4, `internal/cli` 75.3→75.4, `internal/cli/uikit` 98.8 flat). **Known debt (transparent, deferred to a follow-up)**: `AC-FAG-017`'s skip-count sub-assertion greps `^--- SKIP` on non-verbose `go test` output, which never emits that line and is therefore structurally pinned at 0 — the underlying requirement (no new test skips) was verified directly instead (56 skips at both merge-base and HEAD, empty named-skip diff in both directions); `AC-FAG-008`'s `install`-guidance sub-assertion passes with M1 alone (zero M2 change), though its other two sub-assertions (exit code, absence of `no findings`) correctly discriminate; `AC-FAG-015`'s prose mislabels which of its two commands is the actual registration guard. The largest residual risk (sync-audit Gap 3): whether Claude Code's runtime actually surfaces `HookOutput.SystemMessage` to the user on a PreToolUse `allow` decision was confirmed on the wire (the field is emitted correctly) but not against a live Claude Code session. `MOAI_LOG_FORMAT` remains unwired — explicitly out of scope per `spec.md` §C — and is now a visibly misleading knob rather than an invisibly inert one, a candidate for a follow-up SPEC. Route B (PR-mandatory, this repo's branch-protection override); single sync commit carries the `in-progress → implemented → completed` 3-phase close. 🗿 MoAI

- **[SPEC-CI-FLAKY-STABILIZE-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-CI-FLAKY-STABILIZE-001/spec.md)** — root-caused and fixed two independently-confirmed CI flaky defects (Tier M, 28/28 AC PASS — 0 FAIL, 0 PASS-WITH-DEBT). **(1) cobra lazy-sort data race** (`internal/cli/main_test.go`, `internal/cli/preference/main_test.go` — new) — cobra v1.10.2 sorts `c.commands` and writes `commandsAreSorted` *inside* the `Commands()` accessor itself, so concurrent first-call `t.Parallel()` tests racing on `PreferenceCmd.Commands()` / `rootCmd.Commands()` triggered `WARNING: DATA RACE` (reproduced 20/20 with an isolated, real-global repro test — see progress.md §E.2 M1 — then eliminated 0/20 after the fix, and 0 races across 50 fresh-process `-race` runs each for both packages). The fix adds a `TestMain`-driven recursive warm-up (`warmUpCommandTree`) that walks the full command tree from `rootCmd`/`PreferenceCmd` before `m.Run()`, forcing the lazy sort to complete before any parallel test starts. A reachability-signal guard test (`warmUpDone`, asserted only inside the warm-up function body — never in `TestMain` — so the guard's FAIL path is provably reachable, verified by round-trip: comment out the warm-up call → guard FAILs deterministically, restore → PASSes) replaces the original 0.1.0 sort-state assertion, which was structurally unfalsifiable (cobra always leaves the slice sorted once `Commands()` is called, warm-up or not). An independent sync-audit later found `warmUpDone` incomplete: it proves warm-up was *called*, not that it *traversed* — gutting only the recursive loop body while keeping the `TestMain` call left `warmUpDone == true` yet still reproduced the race (1/20). The guard was strengthened (0.4.0, AC-CFS-004c) with a second, additive signal `warmUpVisited` (incremented once per node the warm-up recursion actually visits) compared against `warmUpTreeSize`, a node count produced by a **deliberately separate** traversal (`countCommandTree`) captured in `TestMain` immediately after warm-up and before `m.Run()`. The separate-traversal + `TestMain`-timing placement surfaced a non-obvious pitfall: the global command tree **grows during the run** — `internal/cli/help_order_test.go` calls `runFang(ctx, rootCmd)`, and cobra's Execute path appends `help` + `completion` + 4 shell children (6 nodes total) via `InitDefaultHelpCmd`/`InitDefaultCompletionCmd`; because that test file sorts before `main_test.go` and runs non-parallel, it executes first. Recomputing the node count *inside* the guard test (rather than capturing it once in `TestMain`) measures the tree *after* this growth and produced a false `visited 182` vs `holds 188` FAIL against a correct implementation — a structural defect, not a tuning issue, resolved by taking both counts at the same point in time. Round-trip verified in both packages: body-gutting → deterministic FAIL (`visited 1` vs `tree 182` in `internal/cli`, vs `tree 3` in `preference`); call-removal → still FAILs on `warmUpDone`; restored → `ok`. A new stdlib-only `go/ast` inventory guard (`internal/cli/inventory_guard_test.go`) statically derives the warm-up reachable-set from `<parent>.AddCommand(<ident>)` edges plus transitive closure and fails if any `t.Parallel()` test calling `.Commands()` on a package-level global receiver falls outside it (round-trip-verified: excluding `githubCmd`'s edge reproduces the exact FAIL this guard is meant to catch). **(2) session registry flock starvation** (`internal/session/registry.go`) — the `withLock` retry loop's fixed 20ms sleep let the same losing goroutine repeatedly lose the race under contention, producing tail-latency spikes (run-phase measurement: baseline max 168.6–539.2ms across 3 runs, 3.2x run-to-run variance) that could exceed the CI `ErrLockTimeout` budget. Replaced with full-jitter exponential backoff (base 5ms, ×2, cap 50ms, `math/rand/v2`, remaining-deadline-clamped). An independent sync-audit re-measured the improvement with 8 pre-fix/post-fix binary pairs run alternately (controlling for machine drift): the direction reproduced — post-fix won 7 of 8 pairs, median max `147.6ms → 132.1ms` (≈ −10%) — but **run-to-run variance did not narrow; it widened** (pre-fix 145.7–169.1ms, 1.16x; post-fix 110.6–155.9ms, 1.41x), which is also what the mechanism predicts: full jitter *adds* randomness, so narrower spread was never a reasonable expectation. The original 3-run-vs-3-run `3.2x → 1.1x` figures (and the `539.2ms` baseline maximum) are a small-sample artifact that did not reproduce under the paired re-measurement. p50 stays effectively unchanged (no added latency on the uncontended path) and the `ErrLockTimeout`/`errors.Is` contract, the unchanged `LockTimeout = 2 * time.Second` default, and the `unix.LOCK_EX|LOCK_NB` flock flags are all verified byte-for-byte unchanged. A new characterization test (`internal/session/registry_starvation_test.go`) records p50/p95/max under contention (asserting only a max-ceiling, never p50/p95, to avoid introducing a second flaky test) and is `-short`-skipped. Full `go test ./...` exit 0 (105 packages); `go vet ./...` clean; `golangci-lint run` 0 new issues (baseline was already 0); cross-platform builds (`darwin_arm64`/`windows_amd64`/`linux_amd64`) all exit 0; coverage delta `internal/session` +0.5pp, `internal/cli`/`internal/cli/preference` unchanged (only `_test.go` files added). The two temporary real-global repro tests used to prove the RED state (M1) are not present in the final diff — their full source and the observed race output are preserved verbatim in `progress.md` §E.2. **Transparent debt (sync-audit F3):** the inventory guard's `warmUpRoots` map is fixed to two directories (`.` and `preference`); other `internal/cli` subpackages (`worktree`, `harness`, `agentlint`) also hold package-level cobra globals but currently have **0 violations** (verified repo-wide — no test file in those packages combines `t.Parallel()` with `.Commands()` today), so the two roots exactly cover today's risk surface — a future parallel `Commands()` test added to an uncovered subpackage would go undetected until the map is updated. **Residual limitation:** `countCommandTree`'s placement in `TestMain` (rather than inside the guard test body) is protected only by a source comment (`DO NOT "simplify" this back into the guard test body`), not by a mechanical check — a "simplifying" refactor that moves the count back into the guard would reintroduce the `182` vs `188` false positive documented above, and a red guard naturally reads as "the guard is wrong," which invites deleting the depth assertion and silently reopening the gap the two-signal design (F1) was built to close. Route B (PR-mandatory, this repo's branch-protection override); single sync commit carries the `in-progress → implemented → completed` 3-phase close. 🗿 MoAI

M7 / LOW 8) in one Tier L effort (21/21 AC PASS — 20 clean + 1 PASS-WITH-DEBT). **(1) ast-grep gate RESTORE, default OFF** (`internal/config/loader_gate.go`, REQ-CAR-011/019/020/021) — a `gate.yaml` config loader makes `AstGrepGate.Enabled` configurable (previously unreachable dead config), with the default-OFF invariant explicitly proven (`NewDefaultGateConfig` + a characterization test); the phantom `utils` ruleDir was removed from `sgconfig.yml` and the go/security rule sets were converted to sg-native `rule:` nested format so config-mode scanning is actually loadable, and the enabled gate degrades gracefully when the `sg` binary is absent; the dead V1 `RunAstGrepGate` code path was deleted, leaving V2 as the sole gate entry. **(2) Distribution-status codification** (REQ-CAR-013/014) — `tool-policy.yaml` is declared dev-only (CLAUDE.local.md §2) with the CLI hard error on a missing file replaced by a graceful one-line notice (`internal/cli/tool_policy.go`); the dangling `mcp-matrix.yaml` template-skill reference was removed/reworded. **(3) M7 test repair + registry reconciliation** (REQ-CAR-009/010, `internal/config/audit_test.go`) — `TestAuditParity` silently `SKIP`ped under plain `go test` because it resolved `.moai/config/sections` relative to the package cwd; it now resolves the repo root via `runtime.Caller` (same pattern as the sibling completeness test) and always PASSes or FAILs. All template-mirrored corrections were applied template-first (`make build` clean, template neutrality/leak guards green). **Debt (transparent)**: **AC-CAR-020(b) PASS-WITH-DEBT** — config-mode scanning loads the curated go/security rule set (functionally equivalent, sg-native format) rather than the literal unconverted root `go-hardcoding.yml`; 2 sg-unparseable multi-node demo rules were dropped (deferred to the backlog `SPEC-ASTGREP-DOGFOOD-CLEANUP-001`, which continues to own 16-language ruleset productization, the 10/17 empty language stubs, and message-language unification). The M1 `db.yaml` dead-key cleanup is intentionally NOT implemented here — it is scoped to the parallel DB-subsystem-removal track per REQ-CAR-015, with a delete-3-keys fallback armed if that track stalls. - **[SPEC-CLI-WIZARD-RESTRUCTURE-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-CLI-WIZARD-RESTRUCTURE-001/spec.md)** — restructured `moai init`'s wizard from a retired Quick/Standard/Advanced 3-mode gate into a single always-shown 3-page flow, recalibrated the model-policy default, and repaired the Page-3 answer→disk persistence chain (Tier L, 19/19 AC PASS, 0 FAIL). **(1) Gate removal + 3-page restructure** (`internal/cli/wizard/questions.go`, REQ-WIZ-001..006) — the former `--standard`/`--advanced` mode gate is gone; `InitQuestions()` now assembles a fixed Page 1 "Basic" (conversation_language, user_name, project_name) + Page 2 "Model & Report" (model_policy, report_format) + Page 3 "Quality & Workflow" (lsp_enabled, enforce_quality, project_mode, design_enabled, claude_design_enabled) shown to every user unconditionally; `claude_design_enabled` remains the sole nested/conditional question (on `design_enabled`). `GitQuestions()` (7 questions) and the 3-way `DefaultQuestions`/`Page3Questions`/`InitQuestions` split are preserved so `moai update --reconfigure` keeps its pre-restructure Git question set unaffected. **(2) Default recalibration to Medium** (`internal/template/model_policy.go`, REQ-WIZ-008/009) — `DefaultModelPolicy` moves from `high` ("Max") to `medium`; `high` remains a fully selectable tier, only the default moved. `lsp_enabled` also flips its default to `true` (opt-out, REQ-WIZ-010), and both the `--model-policy`/`--profile` CLI flags and the reconfigure path continue to take precedence over the wizard answer (AC-WIZ-016, C30/C41 flag-vs-wizard precedence order unchanged). **(3) Page-3 persistence chain repair** (`internal/core/project/initializer_expansion.go`, REQ-WIZ-015/021/022) — Page-3 wizard answers (lsp.enabled, quality.constitution.enforce_quality, project.mode, design.enabled, design.claude_design.enabled) previously were computed but never reliably reached the deployed `.moai/config/sections/*.yaml` files on the deployer path; a new depth-aware `patchYAMLPathValue` line-walking helper patches an existing multi-KB deployed YAML file at an exact `a.b.c` key path in place (preserving every other line, including a second same-named `enabled:` key nested elsewhere in `lsp.yaml`) rather than overwriting the file, and the patch is proven non-destructive (AC-WIZ-010a) against the ~22 KB deployed config set. **(4) Advanced-path full retirement** (REQ-WIZ-018/019) — the dormant Phase-2/advanced-bridge code path (`--advanced` flag, `advanced_bridge` question, `coverage_exemptions_enabled` question, the `harness_profile` wizard question) is removed from `internal/cli/**` and `internal/core/project/**`; `harness_profile` is no longer a wizard question; the pre-existing `--harness-profile` CLI flag is still accepted and parsed, but it currently has **no persisted effect** — its only writer (`writeHarnessProfileYAML`) lost its last production caller in this SPEC (C36, deliberately, so `harness.yaml` is left byte-identical per AC-WIZ-010a) and survives as declared dead code (debt (a) below). **(5) Test reconciliation** (M7) — `TestInit_QualityYAMLContent` and 4 test files' removal-tombstone comments were updated to match the new default/removal set. **19/19 AC PASS** (0 FAIL, 0 PASS-WITH-DEBT at the AC level); coverage `internal/cli/wizard` 93.9%, `internal/core/project` 88.7%, `internal/template` 86.0% (the last figure re-measured on the post-merge tree; run-phase §E.3 recorded 84.9% before the base merge, and each figure is exactly EQUAL to the package's baseline at its own tree point — unchanged, not improved); full `go test ./...` exits 0 (107 packages at run-phase; 105 after the sync-phase merge of `origin/main`, which removed the DB and prompt-cache subsystems); `GOOS=windows GOARCH=amd64 go build ./...` exits 0; `golangci-lint run` 0 issues. **This sync phase additionally rewrote the 4-locale docs-site wizard documentation** (`docs-site/content/{en,ko,ja,zh}/{getting-started/init-wizard.md, getting-started/cli.md, cli-reference/init.md}`, 12 files) to describe the shipped 3-page flow in place of the retired 3-mode narrative, removing every `--standard`/`--advanced` reference (baseline 44 matches across 12 files → 0); `hugo --gc --minify` builds warning-free with 4-locale section/table-row parity confirmed. **Debt (transparent, inherited from run-phase §E.3.1, all zero AC impact)**: (a) `writeHarnessProfileYAML` and its 2 tests remain as dead code pending a plan.md §G amendment authorizing their deletion; (b) `patchYAMLPathValue` is a line-walker, not a full YAML parser — block-scalar (`|`/`>`) values that happen to resemble a matching key path are an untested edge case, with a real YAML round-trip parser recorded as the documented future fix; (c) an absent target key in an existing deployed file is a silent no-op by design (protects hand-edited config) but means a user who deletes `lsp.enabled` from `lsp.yaml` silently stops receiving the wizard answer; (d) `TestInit_QualityYAMLContent` behavior changed for direct-API callers that leave `EnforceQuality` unset (now `false` instead of the old template default `true`) — no production path is exposed to this, since `init.go` always sets the flag explicitly; (e) 4 test files retain removal-tombstone comments naming the now-nonexistent `advanced_bridge`/`harness_profile`/`coverage_exemptions_enabled` literals for audit-trail purposes. **Sync-phase audit (independent, 4-dimension) returned FAIL 0.745 against the Tier L 0.85 threshold — score-driven, with no must-pass criterion failing.** Four documentation/metadata findings were fixed in-place before close: the `sync_commit_sha` marker was bold-and-backtick-wrapped and therefore unreadable by `internal/spec/era.go`, which had misclassified this SPEC as `era: V3R5` and would have exempted it from lifecycle drift detection permanently (now `H-4` / `V3R6`, verified via `moai spec audit --json`); `design.md`/`research.md` were left at `status: in-progress`; the `internal/template` coverage figure was stale; and the `--harness-profile` CHANGELOG sentence overstated the flag's effect. One security finding was also fixed: `--project-mode` reached the project-mode YAML writer unvalidated — an exposure this SPEC introduced, since C32 is what made that write path reachable from `moai init` — so `validateInitFlags` now enforces the `personal`/`team` enum like every sibling flag, with an injection-reproduction test.

**Independent re-audit returned FAIL 0.834** (Functionality 0.88 / Security 0.82 / Craft 0.84 / Consistency 0.80 — harmonic mean, still below the 0.85 Tier L bar) and surfaced a second cluster of findings the first round's fixes had not reached: the docs-site pages this SPEC's own sync phase rewrote still taught the inert `--harness-profile` flag in the non-interactive example, `init.go`'s Page-3 override comment was stale, the `enterprise` project-mode value the S1 validator fix (`0e34ac7a3`) newly rejected was still documented in the deployed `project.yaml.tmpl` template, the S2 debt row misstated `internal/atomicfile.Replace` as unused, the coverage sentence claimed "above baseline" when the run-phase and post-merge figures were in fact identical, the cited sync-audit report path was untracked, and three live findings (a stale `init.go:382` comment, the docs-site LSP-default contradiction, and an unbounded `os.ReadFile` in the patch path) were absent from this section's resolution tables.

This repair pass closes that second cluster. **(6) `--enable-lsp` non-interactive default now matches the wizard** — `internal/cli/init.go`'s `LSPEnabled` field reads `getBoolFlagWithDefault(cmd, "enable-lsp", true)` instead of the bare `getBoolFlag`, so `moai init --non-interactive` (no flags) now persists `lsp.enabled: true`, matching the wizard's interactive default; an explicit `--enable-lsp=false` still overrides it unchanged. This closes the `--enable-lsp` non-interactive-default finding, which the previous revision of this entry listed as deferred debt (f); that debt item is removed rather than renumbered, so no `(f)` remains in the list below. As the same one-line fix, it also restores the truth of the `init.go` Page-3 comment and removes the docs-site contradiction between `init-wizard.md`'s "enabled (Yes)" and `cli.md`/`cli-reference/init.md`'s "(default: false)" (both now read "(default: true)"). **(7) docs-site `--harness-profile` disclosure** — the non-interactive CI example in all 4 locales of `init-wizard.md` no longer includes the inert `--harness-profile default \` line, and the 8 flag-table rows across `cli.md`/`cli-reference/init.md` now state the flag is accepted but currently has no persisted effect, matching the CHANGELOG (debt (a), listed in the debt paragraph above). **(8) `project.yaml.tmpl` regression fix** — the `personal`/`team`/`enterprise` project-mode enum documented in the deployed config template regressed with the S1 validator (`0e34ac7a3`, which rejects `enterprise`, a value the template still documented and the CLI previously accepted); `580d49959` drops `enterprise` from the template's own comments so the documented enum matches the validator, since no code path consumes that value. **(9) Audit-record corrections** (this bullet + `progress.md` §G) — the S2 debt claim now states `internal/atomicfile.Replace` has 10 production call sites across 7 files (`internal/cli/preference/filestore.go`, `internal/harness/tier/tier.go`, `internal/migration/version.go`, `internal/hook/handoff/persist.go`, `internal/goal/state.go`, `internal/session/registry.go`, `internal/session/store.go` ×4) rather than "unused" — it is the *config writers* in `initializer_expansion.go` that do not route through it; and the two sync-audit reports (round 1 + re-audit) are committed to `.moai/reports/sync-audit/` so the `progress.md` §G citation resolves to a real path (siblings in that directory are already tracked; it is not gitignored).

- **[SPEC-SUBAGENT-NESTING-DOCTRINE-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-SUBAGENT-NESTING-DOCTRINE-001/spec.md)** — corrected the subagent-nesting doctrine across the harness to the Claude Code v2.1.217 reality and added an opt-in read-only nesting pilot to `sync-auditor` (Tier M, 16/16 AC — 15 clean + 1 PASS-WITH-DEBT). **(1) Doctrine correction** (REQ-SND-002..011, 7 surfaces: `CLAUDE.md` §4/§14, `agent-authoring.md` §Agent(agent_type) Restrictions/§Fork Subagents/§Tool Permissions, `agent-patterns.md` §Deprecated, `orchestration-mode-selection.md` §Mode 6) — the prior doctrine stated nesting was gated solely by the `Agent` tool being present in a subagent's `tools` list; this understated the actual v2.1.217 double guarantee — nesting requires BOTH the `Agent` tool AND `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENT_SPAWN_DEPTH` > 0, and the runtime default is OFF since v2.1.217, with concurrency caps of 20 concurrent / 200 per-session when enabled. All 7 surfaces and their `internal/template/templates/` mirrors were corrected (Template-First), verified stale-phrasing-gone AND new-facts-present (delta, not presence-only). **(2) sync-auditor read-only nesting pilot** (REQ-SND-013/014/017/019/021, `.claude/agents/moai/sync-auditor.md` + template mirror) — `sync-auditor` gains the `Agent` tool and an opt-in per-dimension child-verifier pattern (Functionality/Security/Craft/Consistency), gated entirely by the `CLAUDE_CODE_MAX_SUBAGENT_SPAWN_DEPTH` env var (absent from the distributed `settings.json.tmpl` — shipped default stays flat, single-level); spawned children are read-only verifiers and never own the binding PASS/FAIL verdict (verdict ownership stays with `sync-auditor` itself), and the other 10 retained agents carry no `Agent` in `tools` (unaffected). **Debt (transparent)**: **AC-SND-016 PASS-WITH-DEBT** — a literal whole-tree grep for the env-var name across `internal/template/templates/` returns 8 (the token is required in doctrine-mirror prose by AC-SND-001/002/003), which is incompatible with a strict "0 occurrences anywhere" reading of REQ-SND-019; the requirement's actual load-bearing invariant — the env var is absent from the distributed `settings.json.tmpl` so the shipped default remains flat — is fully satisfied (0 occurrences there). `make build` regenerated `catalog.yaml` (agent-hash cascade only); `go test ./internal/template/...` exit 0 (mirror-parity + neutrality guards green); no Go production code touched. 🗿 MoAI

- **[SPEC-MODEL-PROFILE-MATRIX-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-MODEL-PROFILE-MATRIX-001/spec.md)** — replaced the two-axis `plan_type × performance_tier` model-routing design with a single per-agent-group `profile` matrix (`max`/`medium`/`low`), moving consumption from shipped-agent frontmatter mutation to runtime-arg spawn injection (Tier L, 5-file plan set, 23/25 AC PASS — 21 clean + 2 PASS-WITH-DEBT — all 7 must-pass AC PASS). **(1) Config schema** (`internal/config/profile.go`, REQ-MPM-001..008) — `llm.profile` closed-set enum (`max`/`medium`/`low`), `EffectiveProfile()` resolving `llm.profile` → legacy `performance_tier` read-time alias (`high`→`max`) → default `medium` (DECISION-002), and `llm.agent_overrides` (`{model, effort}` per agent) with closed-set validation. **(2) Matrix A Go SSOT** (`internal/template/profile_matrix.go`) — the user-confirmed 6-group × 3-profile matrix (spec/auditors, develop, advisor, design/harness/e2e, docs, git) plus `ResolveAgentModelEffort` (override → profile-cell → default precedence) and the `Explore`/unmatched-agent `inherit` sentinel. **(3) `moai model profile [--json]` resolver** (`internal/cli/model.go`, REQ-MPM-025) — a new read-only CLI accessor: MODEL is emitted as the `[1m]`-safe per-spawn runtime arg (the super-advisor pattern, DECISION-001); EFFORT is emitted as documented intent (frontmatter default / GLM overlay / Workflow-prompt steering) since the Agent tool does not accept a per-spawn effort override for named subagents. Applies the GLM overlay (model-alias remap via `llm.glm.models` + `CollapseClaudeEffortToGLM`) when the session backend is GLM. **(4) Selection surfaces** — `moai init --profile <max|medium|low>` flag (persists `llm.profile`, `--plan-type` retired) and the `moai web` Model Policy console profile selector (persists `llm.profile`; the `plan_type` UI surface and read-only display are removed). **(5) `ApplyTierProfile` retirement** — the 66-cell `tierProfiles` map and all 4 production frontmatter-mutation call sites (`initializer.go`, `update.go` ×2, `web/agentfm.go`'s `applyPerfTierEdits`) are retired; the 8 shipped `.claude/agents/moai/*.md` files are restored to lint-canonical `model: inherit` + doc-canonical effort. **(6) Migration** — a legacy `llm.yaml` (`plan_type` + `claude_models` + `performance_tier`) loads without error and resolves to the correct profile; write-time (`template.ApplyProfile`) strips the retired `plan_type`/`claude_models` keys on the next save. **Debt (transparent)**: **AC-MPM-013/014 PASS-WITH-DEBT** — the config-layer `agent_overrides` support and the `moai model profile` matrix render are in place, but the pre-existing `moai web` agentfm surface (SPEC-WEB-CONSOLE-011) still renders/edits per-agent frontmatter rather than the profile matrix — UI-wiring only, neither AC is must-pass. `go build ./...` + `GOOS=windows GOARCH=amd64 go build ./...` exit 0; full `go test ./...` 0 failures; golangci-lint 0 new issues; `HaikuResidualRule` and all existing config CI guards green. - **[SPEC-CLI-TUX-V3-004](.moai/specs/SPEC-CLI-TUX-V3-004/spec.md)** — `doctor`/`status`/`spec view` surface polish + fmt.Print ratchet series-final gate (CLI TUX modernization M4, Tier M, 14/14 AC PASS — 13 clean + 1 PASS-WITH-DEBT). **(1) glamour markdown rendering** (`internal/cli/glamour_style.go`, REQ-TUX4-004/005) — `github.com/charmbracelet/glamour@v1.0.0` introduced as the shared render gateway (`renderMarkdown`/`renderTreeMarkdown`/`glamourRender`/`markdownRichEnabled`), styled entirely from `tui.Theme` tokens (zero new hex literals); rich glamour render fires only on a character-device TTY with `NO_COLOR` unset, all other destinations (pipes, CI, golden-test buffers) get byte-stable plain markdown passthrough at a fixed 100-col word-wrap. `status.go` and `spec_view.go` both re-wired through this gateway, preserving every data field (project name, ADK version, config path, SPEC count, tree structure). **(2) doctor live progress + result tables** (`internal/cli/doctor_render.go`, REQ-TUX4-001/002/003) — a non-intrusive `checkObserver` seam (`runGroupedChecksObserved`) emits one `printer.Step` per check on stderr during `moai doctor` (TTY: in-place erase-line; ### Changed

- **init wizard: auto-detect git mode, drop redundant Git questions** (`d74a2aaff`, #1113) — `init.go` never read 3 of the 7 Git-question answers (`GitHubToken`/`GitLabToken`/`GitLabUsername`, persisted only by `update.go`) and never checked `IsGhAuthenticated`, prompting for a token even when `gh auth` was already set up. The question set now splits into `DefaultQuestions` (init, Git group removed), `GitQuestions` (the 7 Git questions), and `ReconfigureQuestions` (both, in the original order for `moai update`). `git_strategy.mode` is now resolved from the repository instead of asked (no remote → manual, ≥1 remote → personal, provider from the origin host); explicit `--git-mode`/`--git-provider` flags still take precedence, and new team projects now need `--git-mode team` or `moai web` to opt into the team profile.

- **docs-site and README content refresh** — README restructured around a single tokenomics thesis across all four locales with regenerated infographics (`dd0a6ce72`, `e2c8ec625`); docs-site gained 6 section concept infographics × 4 locales (`ee89a19a1`) and restored 4-locale table-of-contents parity (`670d2665d`). Visual/structural content only — no functional change.

### Fixed

- **Windows portability — 71 test failures across 17 packages, 3 production defects repaired** (#1114, #1115, #1116) — a three-round sweep closed every Windows-only CI failure, clearing failures that had long been carried as non-blocking debt (`Test (windows-latest)` was pinned to `continue-on-error`). Two of the failure archetypes were genuine production defects, not test hygiene: **(1)** `projectSlug` (`internal/hook/session_end.go`) mapped `/`, `\`, and `.` to `-` but not `:`, so a Windows absolute path (`C:\Users\...`) produced an illegal path component (`C:-Users-...`) and every derived `~/.claude/projects/{slug}` `mkdir` failed, taking the whole preference/memory subsystem down on Windows; **(2)** `os.Rename` cannot atomically replace an open or existing destination on Windows (unlike POSIX `rename(2)`), breaking the write-temp-then-rename idiom duplicated across 9 unshared helpers — a new `internal/atomicfile` package provides a build-tagged Windows-safe `Replace` (plain `os.Rename` passthrough on non-Windows), migrated at 6 call sites (harness/tier, session/registry, migration/version, cli/preference/filestore, hook/handoff/persist, goal/state); **(3)** handoff consume relied on `os.Rename` failing for the losing side to enforce single-winner claim semantics — a guarantee POSIX provides and Windows does not — so two concurrent sessions could both consume the same pending handoff record, fixed with an explicit `O_CREATE|O_EXCL` claim gate (`atomicfile.Claim`) plus an in-gate re-check. An earlier round (`d4af3fc5e`) fixed a related rename-retry (`writeFileAtomic` sharing-violation) and a port-probe `EADDRINUSE` matching gap that fed into the same effort. The release gate no longer tolerates a Windows regression: `release-pr-multi-os.yml` drops the `continue-on-error` pin previously applied to the windows leg, so every OS now blocks the release. Verified on darwin: `go test ./...` exit 0 (107 packages), `-race` clean on `internal/hook`/`internal/cli`, native and `GOOS=windows GOARCH=amd64` builds exit 0 — Windows runtime behavior itself remains CI-only verified. 🗿 MoAI

- **[SPEC-HOOK-FAILURE-CLASSIFY-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-HOOK-FAILURE-CLASSIFY-001/spec.md)** — `PostToolUseFailure` handler now classifies nested `tool_response` errors and resolves `session_id` from `transcript_path` when absent (fixes #1089; Tier S, 8/8 AC PASS). **(1) Nested-error classification** (`internal/hook/post_tool_failure.go`, REQ-HFC-001/002/003) — `classifyError` previously read only the top-level `input.Error` field; it now also inspects nested `ToolResponse` payloads (`error` / `stderr` keys) so a `PermissionDenied`/`TimeoutError`/etc. category is still assigned when the failure signal is nested one level deeper. The pre-existing top-level-`Error` classification path and the ordered category matcher are unchanged (aggregation widens the haystack only; no new `ErrorCategory` added). **(2) `session_id` resolution from `transcript_path`** (`internal/hook/trace_session.go`, REQ-HFC-004) — `resolveTraceSessionID` derives the session UUID from `filepath.Base(transcript_path)` (stripping `.jsonl`, validated against the RFC 4122 UUID regex) when `session_id` is absent or the pre-existing `"unknown"` bug-#541 substitution fired, wired at the trace-filename binding site in `internal/hook/registry.go` `Dispatch`; unresolvable cases keep the original `"unknown"` fallback unchanged. **(3) Malformed/absent `ToolResponse` resilience** (REQ-HFC-005/006) — malformed nested payloads (bad JSON, bare string, array, absent, empty object) no longer error or panic; the handler falls back to a content-free but non-empty excerpt (500-char error text truncated at 200 runes + `...`). Full suite `go test ./...` exit 0 (106 packages); `go vet`/`golangci-lint` 0 issues; subagent-boundary grep 0 matches; touched functions 100% covered (package-wide `internal/hook` 83.6% baseline unchanged, pre-existing unrelated handlers). Run-phase Route A main-direct (Hybrid Trunk, PR #1102); single sync commit carries the `in-progress → implemented → completed` 3-phase close. 🗿 MoAI

- **cli: strip retired v2 permission deny rules on v2→v3 upgrade** (`ea1b29a68`, fixes #1101) — the v2→v3 clean-reinstall preserved `settings.json` wholesale (no embedded base exists for a 3-way merge), so 12 retired v2-era `deny` entries (`Write`/`Grep`/`Glob` × `./secrets/**`, `~/.ssh/**`, `~/.aws/**`, `~/.config/gcloud/**`) survived every upgrade and produced Claude Code startup warnings each session. A one-shot migration now removes deny entries exactly matching the known retired v2 template strings (exact-string match only, so genuine user customizations are untouched; unknown keys preserved via a `map[string]any` round-trip); a v3-clean file and repeat runs are byte-for-byte no-ops.

- **template: quote `statusLine` path on non-Windows platforms** (`c29c8f5e7`, fixes #1096) — the macOS/Linux branch of `settings.json.tmpl`'s `statusLine` command left `$CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` unquoted, so a project whose absolute path contains spaces word-split the command and the statusbar silently rendered nothing. Quoted to match the Windows branch.

- **template: ship `labels.yml` referenced by the label-sync workflow** (`58527b3e6`, refs #1094) — the distributed `label-sync.yml` workflow declared `.github/labels.yml` as its config source, but the template set never shipped that file, so the workflow was inert and errored on dispatch in every user project. Adds a neutral 3-axis label set (`type:*` / `priority:*` / `status:*` + GitHub-standard functional labels).

- **cli: dedupe banner v-prefix and persist language/name on `update` reconfigure** (`01675965e`) — the banner tolerated only an unprefixed version string, so `version.GetVersion()` (`"v3.0.0"`) rendered as `"vv3.0.0"` in the CLI pill; `applyWizardConfig` now also persists `ConversationLang` to `language.yaml` and `UserName` to `user.yaml` during `moai update` reconfigure (previously only Git fields were saved).

- **spec: era-aware lint demotion for grandfathered/terminal SPECs** (`dd644b5e0`) — `moai spec lint --strict` failed CI with 38 errors, nearly all on SPECs predating the structural rules it enforces. The era classifier (same SSOT as `moai spec audit`) now demotes `MissingExclusions`/`FrontmatterInvalid` findings to advisory on grandfather-era SPECs (V2.x/V3R2-R4/V3R5) and on terminal-status SPECs (`completed`/`superseded`/`archived`/`rejected`) — still visible, no longer failing; active modern-era SPECs keep full enforcement. `moai spec lint --strict` now exits 0 (0 errors, 62 advisory warnings).

- **rules: narrow `paths` gates to `SKILL.md` to stop per-command rule loads** (`bbec41b3a`, fixes #1099) — `prompting-best-practices.md` (`**/.claude/skills/**`) and `native-invocation-model.md` (`**/.claude/skills/moai/**`) gated on the skills directory rather than `SKILL.md`, so every `/moai` command reading its own workflow body loaded 9.3KB/13.5KB of authoring rules per invocation it never needed.

- **[SPEC-V3R6-LINK-FIX-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-V3R6-LINK-FIX-001/spec.md)** — fixed broken repo links, `modu-ai/moai-adk-go` → `modu-ai/moai-adk` (`CONTRIBUTING.md`, `CONTRIBUTING.ko.md`, docs-site) and a stale Anthropic docs URL, `docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/claude-code` → `code.claude.com/docs/en`, across all four README locales (`30694b913`).

- **docs-site: repair single-page layout collapse** (`fa4152261`, `98471f0be`) — four defects found via live browser inspection on the doc-detail layout: a duplicate table-of-contents rail (the legacy right-rail partial rendered alongside the page's own left rail), invisible hero text (brand CSS `h1`/link colors overrode the hero's white text on its green gradient band), a grid track that could not shrink and pushed the rail past the viewport, and a zero-date (`0001.01.01`) render on pages lacking a frontmatter date.

- **[SPEC-DOCS-WTRGO-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-DOCS-WTRGO-001/spec.md)** — corrected the worktree docs across all four locales (en/ko/ja/zh) where `moai worktree go` was wrongly presented as a navigation command; `go` prints the worktree path to stdout and does not change directory, so bare Pattern A/B/C occurrences and Mermaid diagram labels are rewritten to the correct shell idiom `cd "$(moai worktree go SPEC-ID)"`. Also documented `moai worktree switch` in the command-reference table (it prints the worktree location; does not `cd`) and corrected that command's misleading CLI help text in `internal/cli/worktree/switch.go`. 🗿 MoAI

- Internal/CI-only (no user-visible behavior change): dependency bumps (Go modules `5bfd274f9`/`53c440f8b`, GitHub Actions `783fa0bcc`/`c223db587`/`013123d21`); flaky-test repairs (LSP singleflight barrier `409c7738f`, harness observe-stop time-bomb seed `ca879fb0d`, IT04 frozen-guard/brand-dir `f1d3f1946`); a non-reproduced characterization probe for #1094 gitignore-merge (`3c03d5061`); a `paths-filter` dead-zone fix for workflow-only PRs (`851206f25`) and a `scripts/release.sh` CHANGELOG-extraction regex fix (`ede5ad20d`), both maintainer-tooling only; `moai agent lint` local-copy effort-drift cleanup (`6b4937eca`, template mirrors were already correct); and this repository's own branch-protection/PR-mandatory policy tightening (`6fc5dd2dc`).

## [3.0.0] - 2026-07-20

### Summary

v3.0.0 is the first stable (GA) release of the MoAI-ADK v3 line, closing the arc that began after **v2.14.0** (2026-04-24) and stabilized through the `rc1`–`rc12` pre-releases. The headline of v3 is a **tokenomics-first agentic workflow**: MoAI now reasons about token cost as an explicit budget rather than an afterthought. The major stacks that landed and matured across the window:

- **Tokenomics stack** — per-agent/per-phase token accounting, Tier×Phase model routing, `plan_type` profiles that map a SPEC's scope to a model/effort tier, and a **Token Circuit Breaker** (a Stop-hook token-budget guard that halts a runaway autonomous loop before it burns the budget).
- **Self-evolving harness + goal engine** — the `/moai goal` autonomous-continuation loop (a fresh model evaluates a completion condition each turn-end) and the `/moai loop` diagnostic sweep, together with the recursive harness-learning ladder that proposes delegation-map improvements from observed routing.
- **Consolidated 11-agent catalog** — 10 MoAI-custom managers/auditors/builders plus the Anthropic built-in `Explore`; references to archived agent names are rejected at spawn, replaced by per-spawn `Agent(general-purpose)` with a domain whitelist.
- **CG Mode** — a Claude-leader + GLM-teammate tmux topology for cost-optimized, implementation-heavy work.
- **TUX v3 CLI modernization** — `moai init` / `moai update` redesign on bubbletea/huh v2, a change-preview TUI, and the `internal/cli/printer.Printer` stdout=data / stderr=status migration.
- **Documentation + i18n rebuild** — the `adk.mo.ai.kr` docs-site "Claude Warm Editorial" redesign, 4-locale (en/ko/ja/zh) content, and a value-centered README restructure across all four locales.
- **Security hardening** — the `security.yaml` surface, the Bash risk-amplifier doctrine (destructive-primitive confirmation, compound-command soft cap), and the verification-claim integrity invariant (no unobserved verification-or-defect claim).

Per-pre-release detail for each theme lives in the `v3.0.0-rc1`…`v3.0.0-rc12` entries below and in the referenced SPEC files.

### Breaking Changes

- **Major version bump** — `v2.x` → `v3.0.0` (SemVer major). Existing projects should run `moai update` to reconcile templates before upgrading the binary.
- **Agent catalog consolidated to 11 retained agents** — references to archived agent names (e.g. `manager-strategy`, `expert-backend`, `researcher`) are now **rejected at spawn**; the retained-agent replacement pattern (per-spawn `Agent(general-purpose)` with a domain whitelist, or one of the 11 retained agents) is documented in `archived-agent-rejection.md`.
- **Agent Teams static-orchestration layer retired** — a forced `--team` / `--mode team` emits `MODE_TEAM_UNAVAILABLE` and falls back to sub-agent mode. The native Claude Code teammate runtime (`moai cg` GLM panes, `worktree --team`) is unaffected.
- **Context7 MCP dependency retired** — `mcp__context7__*` removed from every `allowed-tools` and the settings ask-list; library-documentation lookups now use the WebSearch/WebFetch fallback strategy.
- **`/moai e2e` replaced** — the web-only E2E subcommand was retired and revived as a multi-platform (web / mobile / desktop / desktop-native) subsystem driven by the new `e2e-tester` agent.

### Added

- **[SPEC-DESIGN-DOCSV2-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-DESIGN-DOCSV2-001/spec.md)** — docs-site (`adk.mo.ai.kr`) migrated to the 모두의AI Design System v2 (Epic DESIGN-V2, SPEC-1 of 2; Tier L, 30/30 AC PASS — 25 MUST + 5 SHOULD). **(1) Token unification (M1-M2)** — the prior 3-layer warm-cream token stack (`moai-brand.css` FROZEN + `moai-docs-tokens.css` Clay/Cream/Ink + `moai-docs-theme.css` remap) is collapsed into a single v2-native SSOT: achromatic neutral scale `#f4f4f4 → #060606`, single signature green `#3d7d5f`/`#316750`/`#265240`, solid de-emphasized `--gradient-signature` (no `135deg` gradient), Pretendard-only Korean typography (MaruBuri `@font-face` removed), and a two-token mono split (`--font-mono` JetBrains Mono + `--font-code` Goorm Sans Code). **(2) Layout (M3)** — home/listing adopts the round3 docs-index prototype (hero + sticky filter pills + featured + 4:5 grid) and doc-detail adopts the round3 docs-detail prototype (3-column + sticky TOC + read-progress + next-doc CTA), with the existing i18n/menu/⌘K-search infrastructure fully preserved. **(3) Mermaid v2 palette (M4)** — `foot.html` themeVariables repointed to the v2 palette (`primaryColor #eef4f0`, `lineColor #9fa0a0`), retaining mermaid v10. **(4) Mascot expansion (M5)** — 6 MoAI-Mascot PNGs installed to `static/mascots/`, surfaced on 3 emotional surfaces (home hero, 404, empty-section) via the promoted `mascot.html` shortcode; data/form/checkout surfaces remain mascot-free. **(5) Component v2 recipes (M6)** — buttons migrate to a solid-fill pill recipe (`border-radius: 9999px`, no neo-brutalist offset shadow) and cards migrate to a hairline-border + soft-shadow surface recipe (`border: 1px solid #d1d1d1`, `box-shadow: 0 2px 4px rgba(6,6,6,0.06)`), replacing the rounded-border + left-color-accent pattern; v2 shadow/motion tokens (`--shadow-sm/md/lg`, `--dur-*`, `--ease-*`) are consumed throughout. **(6) 4-locale parity + build gate (M7)** — the last hardcoded-Korean UI strings (10 `rail_*` doc-rail keys) are promoted to i18n across ko/en/ja/zh, a `.prose-kr` Korean-readability class is added, and `hugo --minify --gc` closes warning-free (KO 153 / EN 150 / JA 139 / ZH 150 pages, held identical across every milestone). **Sync-phase**: `docs-site/static/moai-brand.css` is re-stamped FROZEN with the v2 token vocabulary as the new frozen baseline (comment-only; no token values changed). **Known doc inconsistency** (flagged, not corrected — forbidden ownership crossing): acceptance.md §A headline states "20 MUST / 10 SHOULD"; the actual Severity-column tally is 25 MUST + 5 SHOULD. Run-phase Route A main-direct (Hybrid Trunk, no PR); single sync commit carries the `in-progress → completed` 3-phase close. 🗿 MoAI

- **[SPEC-CLI-TUX-V3-005](.moai/specs/SPEC-CLI-TUX-V3-005/spec.md)** — AC-TUX3-020 Printer migration: `fmt.Print*` ratchet succession in non-test `internal/cli` sources (Tier M, 5/5 AC PASS — 4 MUST + AC-TUX3-023 SHOULD). Mapping: JSON output + human-format display → `p.Data` (stdout, byte-identical to the prior sequential `fmt.Print*`); status/success messages → `p.Info`/`p.Success` (re-routed stdout→stderr per the stdout=machine-data / stderr=human-status convention); the multi-line human-format blocks (`printPhaseStateHuman`, migration status display) compose into a single `p.Data(strings.Join(lines, "\n"))` string so scripted consumers are unaffected (DECISION 2026-07-14). 3 gap-sites EXCLUDED per the M1 architecture gate: `uikit/banner.go` 12 (TUI lipgloss render — Printer interface not extended), `branch_protection.go` 1 (ttyConfirmer dead code, `nolint:unused`), `worktree/new.go` 1 (commented-out godoc example). Behavior preservation pinned by 3 new characterization-test files (`state_m2_test.go`, `migration_m3_test.go`, `tmux_integration_m4_test.go`) with golden-master stdout/stderr assertions; `internal/cli` coverage 74.1% (AC-TUX3-023 SHOULD-PASS). - **[SPEC-CLI-TUX-V3-003](.moai/specs/SPEC-CLI-TUX-V3-003/spec.md)** — `moai update` decomposed into 5 subpackages + change-preview TUI (CLI TUX modernization M3, Tier L, 18/20 AC PASS-WITH-DEBT: 18 clean + AC-TUX3-018 partial + AC-TUX3-020 debt). **(1) 5-subpackage decomposition** (`internal/cli/update/{plan,backup,deploy,merge,report}`, AC-TUX3-004) — the 3,276-LOC `update.go` is refactored so analysis/classification lives in `plan` (8 funcs incl. the `isUserOwnedNamespace` ANCHOR predicate), config backup/restore + YAML 3-way merge helpers in `backup` (8 funcs + 5-func merge cluster, with a nil-safe `MergeFallbackRecorder` callback seam to avoid a circular import), clean leaf deploy ops in `deploy` (3 funcs: `CleanMoaiManagedPaths`, `MigrateLegacyMemoryDir`, `ScaffoldEvolutionDir`), merge orchestration in `merge` (4 funcs), and hook-review guidance emission in `report`; `update.go` drops to ~1,730 LOC retaining cobra wiring + the 3 template-sync/agency-migration funcs that are entangled with root-cli helpers (callback-seam extraction documented as deferred debt). 6 guard-test files across the namespace-protection family take ONLY import-path/qualifier adjustments — assertion bodies UNMODIFIED (REQ-TUX3-005); `internal/merge/confirm.go` is untouched by the decomposition. **(2) Shared classification model** (`internal/cli/update/class.go`, AC-TUX3-001/002/014) — a single `ChangeClass` enum (4 values: Add / Update / PreserveUserOwned / Conflict) + `Classify(relPath, exists, conflict, isUserOwned)` is the one shared type consumed by both the TUI preview and the text fallback; the preserve class delegates entirely to the injected `UserOwnedPredicate` (no parallel heuristic), and renders the `"preserved (user-owned)"` label in both surfaces. **(3) Change-preview TUI** (`preview.go` + `preview_tui.go` + `preview_fallback.go`, AC-TUX3-008/009/010) — built on `charm.land/bubbletea/v2` (table + viewport) and `charm.land/bubbles/v2` (landed by SPEC-CLI-TUX-V3-002); a `confirmViaPreview` single-entry convergence at `update.go:1609` routes the prior call sites through it, behavior-preserving (non-TTY confirm path mirrors the old `tea.Run` failure; TTY preview + `--yes`/non-TTY fallback produce the SAME classification decision). The TUI shows per-class counts, an every-file row, a row-select → diff-viewport, and Esc-returns-to-table; the text fallback carries zero ANSI under `NO_COLOR` or when piped, and a non-TTY summary form. **(4) confirm.go v2 promotion** (M3e, AC-TUX3-011/012/013) — the prior `confirm.go` v1 (huh v1 + bubbles v1) is promoted to a v2 bubbles-list form with the unified outcome card; the 3 `@MX:DEBT`/`@MX:CEILING`/`@MX:UPGRADE` annotations at lines 871-873 are RESOLVED (removed). **(5) M3b characterization safety net** (`update_characterization_test.go`, 8 tests / 14 subtests) — pins observable behavior of the UNDECOMPOSED `update.go` that the M3d decomposition MUST preserve (flag surface, mutual exclusion, golden classify values, namespace predicate superset, moai-managed classification, fast-path version comparison); remained green across every decomposition milestone. **(6) Coherence integration test** (`coherence_test.go`, AC-TUX3-016) — bridges `update.Classify` to `isUserOwnedNamespace` (REQ-TUX3-002 reachability) + asserts the `preserved (user-owned)` label. Full suite (`go test ./... -count=1`) exit 0; cross-platform build (darwin/windows amd64) exit 0; `golangci-lint` 0 issues on SPEC-003 scope (AC-TUX3-019); namespace guard 5-family (TestSplitHarnessNamespaceNoLeak + cli `Namespace|SecurityM2` + `internal/merge/...`) green UNMODIFIED across all milestones. **Debt (transparent)**: **AC-TUX3-018 partial** — 3/5 subpackages ≥85% coverage (report 92.9% · plan 95.0% · merge 90.3% ✓); `deploy` 74.7% and `backup` 75.2% sit below the 85% target on error-handling paths (manager-develop judged the gap reasonable for flaky-risk paths; ratchet to 85% is follow-up scope). **AC-TUX3-020 debt** — the `fmt.Print*` ratchet in non-test `internal/cli` sources holds at the baseline 38 (no regression); migrating the remaining call sites to the `internal/cli/printer` abstraction is a larger follow-up (Printer migration). acceptance.md AC-TUX3-009/010 `-run` regexes use `\|` (RE2 literal-pipe escape, not alternation) and AC-TUX3-015 names a `UserAssetPreservation` test absent from the suite — both are acceptance.md wording fixes owed to manager-spec (forbidden ownership crossing; NOT edited in sync-phase). 🗿 MoAI
