### Fixed

- **[SPEC-V3R6-V2-V3-CLEAN-REINSTALL-002](.moai/specs/SPEC-V3R6-V2-V3-CLEAN-REINSTALL-002/spec.md)** — repaired two `moai update` v2→v3 clean-reinstall regressions introduced by the parent SPEC (`SPEC-V3R6-V2-V3-CLEAN-REINSTALL-001`): **#1084** (silent data-loss infinite loop) and **#1086** (arbitrary-directory template pollution). Tier M, 10/10 AC PASS (9 MUST + 1 SHOULD cross-platform). **(1) v3-version negative-override** (`internal/cli/v2_detection.go`, REQ-CRR-001/AC-CRR-001) — `probeVersionSignal` now returns a third `v3Confirmed` flag; when `system.yaml` carries a populated `v3.*` version, the new `V2Fingerprint.V3VersionConfirmed` field short-circuits `IsV2` to `false` regardless of Signal 2 (`.agency/`) or Signal 3 (DeprecatedPaths residue) — breaking the loop that reset user-modified `.moai/config/sections/language.yaml`/`design.yaml` to template defaults on every invocation (REQ-CRR-002/003, AC-CRR-002/003). **(2) Positive project-marker precondition** (`internal/cli/update.go`, REQ-CRR-004/005, AC-CRR-004/005) — `moai update` (non-`--binary`) now refuses to run in a directory lacking `.moai/config/sections/system.yaml`, returning a structured `not a moai project` error before `acquireUpdateLock` (the lock itself `MkdirAll`s `.moai/`, so a post-lock gate would still leave an empty directory behind) — an M2-introduced `isMoAIProject` conjunct on the clean-reinstall branch alone was insufficient because Option α's "missing system.yaml ⇒ positive Signal 1" heuristic let a non-project cwd fall through to the v3 file-level sync path, which still deployed the full embedded template tree. **(3) Actual-removal-count log gating** (`internal/cli/update_clean_install.go`, REQ-CRR-006/AC-CRR-006) — the REMOVE-phase log now derives `removedCount` from a pre/post-REMOVE filesystem re-scan diff instead of the 43-entry planned-list length, emitting `Removed N deprecated paths` only when `N > 0` (else `No deprecated paths found to remove`), eliminating the phantom "Removed 10 deprecated paths" log on runs that removed nothing. **(4) `.agency/` migration decoupling** (REQ-CRR-007/AC-CRR-007) — `runMigrateAgency` fires independently of the v2 fingerprint verdict, gated solely on `.agency/` presence, so legacy-asset migration keeps working on v3 projects without re-activating clean-reinstall. Two Reproduction-First tests (`TestReproduction_FingerprintNonConvergence_Issue1084`, `TestReproduction_NonProjectDirectoryPollution_Issue1086`) were confirmed RED against the true pre-fix baseline (`0cdf18e07`) before the fix landed, plus a 3-run idempotency test (`TestRunUpdate_ThreeRunIdempotency_V3Project`, AC-CRR-009) and a cross-platform parity test (`TestFingerprintPredicate_CrossPlatformParity`, AC-CRR-010, S2). The parent SPEC's PRESERVE-inventory enumeration, SHA-256 hash-diff detection, backup-before-removal scheme, and the frozen 43-entry DeprecatedPaths list are all unweakened (`internal/cli/update_preserve_inventory.go` 0 lines changed, AC-CRR-008). 🗿 MoAI

- **[SPEC-V3R6-ZONE-REGISTRY-PACKAGING-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-V3R6-ZONE-REGISTRY-PACKAGING-001/spec.md)** — `.claude/rules/moai/core/zone-registry.md` (115 `CONST-*` entries, consumed at runtime by `moai constitution list/guard/amend/validate`, `moai doctor`, and `moai spec lint`) is now packaged into the embedded template tree, fixing the lone-omission gap where every deployed project (`moai init`/`moai update`) lacked the registry entirely — `moai constitution` failed to load, `moai doctor` downgraded to Warn, and the zone-registry-backed `moai spec lint` dimension was silently skipped (Tier S, GitHub #1090, 6/7 AC PASS + 1 PASS-WITH-DEBT). A neutralized sanitized-pair mirror is added at `internal/template/templates/.claude/rules/moai/core/zone-registry.md` (source file untouched; 2 SPEC-IDs + 2 dates neutralized in the mirror only), preserving all 119 `CONST-V3R*` governance tokens under the default template-neutrality tier (`go test ./internal/template/...` exit 0). The pre-existing strict-tier leak baseline (132 unrelated internal-date occurrences across ~40 other template files) is unchanged with or without the mirror (evidence: 132/132) — out of scope for this fix. 🗿 MoAI

- **[SPEC-UPDATE-REINSTALL-LOOP-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-UPDATE-REINSTALL-LOOP-001/spec.md)** — broke the `moai update` clean-reinstall infinite loop and restored config preservation on the clean-reinstall path (GitHub #1084, Critical, Tier M, 8/8 gating AC PASS — AC-RIL-001..007 + 010; AC-RIL-008/009 SPLIT to follow-up `SPEC-UPDATE-PREFLIGHT-SAFETY-001`). **Root cause** — `.claude/rules/moai/design` was simultaneously a `defs.DeprecatedPaths` entry AND shipped by the v3 embedded template, so the v2-detection "deprecated path present" signal fired perpetually on every v3 project: the clean-reinstall removed the path, the template redeployed it, re-arming the next `moai update` (zero-net-change infinite loop) — a version-format-independent regression the existing `v3.`-prefixed system.yaml override could not suppress. **M2 (commit `94eb1b58a`)** — removed the stale `.claude/rules/moai/design` entry from `defs.DeprecatedPaths` (Category B, total 41→40, Category B 29→28, counts updated atomically in `dirs_test.go` guards + `dirs.go` + `v2_detection.go` header comments); added a build-time regression guard `TestDeprecatedPaths_NoTemplateCollision` asserting `DeprecatedPaths ∩ embedded-template-FS = ∅` (with a negative-path proof that re-inserting a colliding entry FAILs the guard). **M1 (commit `59f52dc8e`)** — restored config preservation on the clean-reinstall path: `.claude/settings.json` user customizations (effortLevel, teammateMode, permissions, non-MoAI hooks) and ALL `.moai/config/sections/*.yaml` files (not only `user.yaml` — issue #1084 explicitly reported `language.yaml`/`design.yaml` loss) now survive a clean-reinstall via the same merge/preserve protection (`backup.RestoreMoaiConfig` + `updatemerge.MergeUserFiles`) the normal `moai update` path already applies, closing the clean-reinstall's lower-protection bypass; a user-configured higher-capability `model` (e.g. `opus`) survives as a by-product of the merge-preserving `settings.json` handling (no template pin removal needed). **Scope note**: pre-flight safety hardening (fail-closed abort on symlink / nested-`.git` before destruction, no-half-migrated-state invariant — AC-RIL-008/009) is SPLIT to the follow-up `SPEC-UPDATE-PREFLIGHT-SAFETY-001` per plan-audit iter-1 decision; this SPEC's scope is the loop-break + config-preservation fix only. Full suite (`go test ./...`) green aside from a pre-existing unrelated `internal/config` failure (stash-verified pre-existing on clean tree); cross-platform build (darwin native + `GOOS=windows GOARCH=amd64`) exit 0. 🗿 MoAI

- **[SPEC-V3R6-DOCTOR-FALSE-SIGNAL-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-V3R6-DOCTOR-FALSE-SIGNAL-001/spec.md)** — repaired a `moai doctor` false-signal cluster sharing one root theme: the doctor conflated mere on-disk PRESENCE of a runtime-generated artifact, or a hand-maintained STALE reference list, with a genuine "configured / known" state (Tier M, 9/9 AC PASS — 8 clean + 1 PASS-WITH-DEBT). **Defect A (GitHub #1087)** — the Harness 5-Layer check (`internal/cli/doctor_harness.go` `runHarnessCheck`) previously decided "harness configured?" from `.moai/harness/` directory EXISTENCE alone, so the FIRST learning-telemetry write (`usage-log.jsonl`, appended by the PostToolUse/Stop/SubagentStop observe hooks) flipped a project that never configured a v4 harness from `CheckOK` to a red `CheckFail` (L2-L5 FAIL, missing baseline files). The check now excludes runtime telemetry artifacts from the "configured" determination — a `.moai/harness/` directory containing ONLY `usage-log.jsonl` still reports `CheckOK` "no harness configured", while a directory containing at least one of the 7 baseline harness files (`main.md`/`plan-extension.md`/`run-extension.md`/`sync-extension.md`/`chaining-rules.yaml`/`interview-results.md`/`README.md`) still runs the full L1-L6 battery unchanged (partial-baseline misconfiguration still correctly FAILs). **Defect B (GitHub #1088)** — the Skills Allowlist check (`internal/cli/doctor_skills.go` `classifySkill`/`checkSkillsAllowlist`) validated `.claude/skills/moai-*` directories against a hand-maintained 22-entry static Go slice that had drifted from the embedded template manifest (27 `moai-*` skills), reporting "10 unknown moai- skill(s) detected (run 'moai update' to sync)" on a project whose skills were JUST installed by that same binary's embedded templates — a misleading hint, since `moai update` cannot fix a drift in the doctor's own hardcoded list. The allowlist is now DERIVED from the authoritative embedded template skills manifest (`internal/template/skills_manifest.go`, new), so a template-fresh project reports zero unknown skills by construction, while a genuinely unrecognized `moai-*` directory (stale or third-party) still classifies `CheckWarn`. **Preservation guard** — both fixes are read-only diagnostics confined to `internal/cli/doctor_harness.go` / `internal/cli/doctor_skills.go` / `internal/cli/doctor.go` plus the new manifest-read helper and tests; `internal/cli/update.go`, `internal/cli/update_clean_install.go`, and `internal/harness/applier.go` are un-diffed, and the `SPEC-V3R6-V2-V3-CLEAN-REINSTALL-002` preservation test suites (namespace / clean-install / preserve families) pass unchanged. Reproduction-first TDD: both defects were RED-confirmed (byte-matching the exact issue reports) on the pre-fix tree, then GREEN post-fix. Full suite (`go test ./...`) and cross-platform build (darwin + windows/amd64) green; per-function coverage on changed funcs 80.8%-98.2%. **PASS-WITH-DEBT (AC-DFS-009)**: 2 pre-existing/flaky test failures outside this SPEC's scope were observed during the full-suite run and are not attributable to either fix. Run-phase Route A main-direct (Hybrid Trunk, no PR); single sync commit carries the `in-progress → completed` 3-phase close. 🗿 MoAI

- Per-pre-release fixes (e.g. the profile-display bug fix) are recorded in the `v3.0.0-rc1`…`v3.0.0-rc12` entries below.

### Installation & Update

- **New install** — `go install github.com/modu-ai/moai-adk/cmd/moai@v3.0.0`, or download a prebuilt binary from the GitHub release assets (asset names carry no `v` prefix per the update checker contract).
- **Existing projects** — run `moai update` to sync the v3.0.0 templates (user-owned files under `.claude/`, `.moai/project/`, `.moai/specs/` are preserved), then `moai update --binary` to upgrade the CLI.
- **Verify** — `moai version` reports `v3.0.0`.

---

## [3.0.0] - 2026-07-20 (한국어)

### 요약

v3.0.0은 MoAI-ADK v3 라인의 첫 정식(GA) 릴리스로, **v2.14.0**(2026-04-24) 이후 시작되어 `rc1`–`rc12` 사전 릴리스를 거쳐 안정화된 개발 구간을 마무리한다. v3의 핵심은 **토크노믹스 우선 에이전트 워크플로우** — MoAI가 토큰 비용을 부차적 요소가 아니라 명시적 예산으로 다룬다는 점이다. 구간 전반에 걸쳐 안착·성숙한 주요 스택은 다음과 같다.

- **토크노믹스 스택** — 에이전트/페이즈별 토큰 회계, Tier×Phase 모델 라우팅, SPEC 범위를 모델·effort 티어에 매핑하는 `plan_type` 프로파일, 그리고 자율 루프가 예산을 소진하기 전에 중단시키는 Stop-훅 토큰 예산 가드 **Token Circuit Breaker**.
- **자기진화 하네스 + goal 엔진** — 매 턴 종료 시 별도 모델이 완료 조건을 평가하는 `/moai goal` 자율 지속 루프와 `/moai loop` 진단 스윕, 그리고 관찰된 라우팅으로부터 위임 맵 개선안을 제안하는 재귀적 하네스 학습 사다리.
- **11-에이전트 카탈로그 통합** — MoAI 커스텀 매니저/감사/빌더 10종 + Anthropic 내장 `Explore`; 아카이브된 에이전트 이름 참조는 spawn 시 거부되고, 도메인 화이트리스트를 가진 per-spawn `Agent(general-purpose)`로 대체된다.
- **CG 모드** — 구현 위주 작업의 비용 최적화를 위한 Claude 리더 + GLM 팀메이트 tmux 토폴로지.
- **TUX v3 CLI 현대화** — bubbletea/huh v2 기반 `moai init` / `moai update` 재설계, 변경 미리보기 TUI, `internal/cli/printer.Printer`의 stdout=데이터 / stderr=상태 마이그레이션.
- **문서 + i18n 재구축** — `adk.mo.ai.kr` docs-site "Claude Warm Editorial" 재설계, 4개 로케일(en/ko/ja/zh) 콘텐츠, 4개 로케일 전반의 가치 중심 README 재구성.
- **보안 강화** — `security.yaml` 표면, Bash 위험 증폭기 독트린(파괴적 프리미티브 확인·복합 명령 소프트 캡), 검증-주장 무결성 불변식(관찰되지 않은 검증-또는-결함 주장 금지).

각 테마의 사전 릴리스별 세부 내용은 아래 `v3.0.0-rc1`…`v3.0.0-rc12` 항목과 참조된 SPEC 파일에 있다.

### 주요 변경 사항

- **메이저 버전 승격** — `v2.x` → `v3.0.0` (SemVer major). 기존 프로젝트는 바이너리 업그레이드 전에 `moai update`로 템플릿을 정합화해야 한다.
- **에이전트 카탈로그 11종으로 통합** — 아카이브된 에이전트 이름 참조는 이제 spawn 시 **거부**된다. 대체 패턴(도메인 화이트리스트를 가진 per-spawn `Agent(general-purpose)` 또는 11종 유지 에이전트 중 하나)은 `archived-agent-rejection.md`에 문서화되어 있다.
- **Agent Teams 정적 오케스트레이션 계층 폐기** — 강제 `--team` / `--mode team`은 `MODE_TEAM_UNAVAILABLE`을 내고 sub-agent 모드로 폴백한다. 네이티브 Claude Code 팀메이트 런타임(`moai cg` GLM 페인, `worktree --team`)은 영향받지 않는다.
- **Context7 MCP 의존성 폐기** — 모든 `allowed-tools`와 settings ask 목록에서 `mcp__context7__*` 제거; 라이브러리 문서 조회는 WebSearch/WebFetch 폴백 전략을 사용한다.
- **`/moai e2e` 교체** — 웹 전용 E2E 서브커맨드를 폐기하고, 새 `e2e-tester` 에이전트가 구동하는 멀티플랫폼(web / mobile / desktop / desktop-native) 서브시스템으로 부활시켰다.

### 추가됨

- **[SPEC-CLI-TUX-V3-005]** — 비테스트 `internal/cli` 소스의 `fmt.Print*` → `internal/cli/printer.Printer` 인터페이스 마이그레이션(24개 호출부, ratchet 38 → 14). 상세는 위 영어 항목 참조.

### 변경됨

- **[SPEC-CLI-TUX-V3-003]** — `moai update`의 5개 서브패키지 분해 + 변경 미리보기 TUI, 이후 AC-TUX3-018 커버리지 완결을 위한 수정 재클로즈. 상세는 위 영어 항목 참조.

### 수정됨

- **[SPEC-V3R6-V2-V3-CLEAN-REINSTALL-002]** — `moai update` v2→v3 클린 재설치 회귀 2건 수정(#1084 무한 루프·데이터 손실, #1086 임의 디렉터리 템플릿 오염). Tier M, 10/10 AC. 상세는 위 영어 항목 참조.
- **[SPEC-V3R6-ZONE-REGISTRY-PACKAGING-001]** — `zone-registry.md`(115 CONST 엔트리)를 임베디드 템플릿 트리에 패키징, 배포 프로젝트의 `moai constitution`·`moai doctor`·`moai spec lint` 로드 실패 수정(Tier S, #1090, 6/7 AC). 상세는 위 영어 항목 참조.
- 프로파일 표시 버그 수정 등 구간별 수정 내역은 아래 `v3.0.0-rc1`…`v3.0.0-rc12` 항목 참조.

### 설치 및 업데이트

- **신규 설치** — `go install github.com/modu-ai/moai-adk/cmd/moai@v3.0.0`, 또는 GitHub 릴리스 자산에서 사전 빌드 바이너리 다운로드(자산 이름은 업데이트 체커 규약에 따라 `v` 접두어 없음).
- **기존 프로젝트** — `moai update`로 v3.0.0 템플릿 동기화(`.claude/`, `.moai/project/`, `.moai/specs/` 하위 사용자 소유 파일은 보존), 이후 `moai update --binary`로 CLI 업그레이드.
- **검증** — `moai version`이 `v3.0.0`을 보고.

## [v3.0.0-rc12] - 2026-07-14

### Added

- **[SPEC-DESKTOP-NATIVE-E2E-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-DESKTOP-NATIVE-E2E-001/spec.md)** — `/moai e2e` gains a `desktop-native` OS-accessibility automation lane, replacing the deferral notice from SPEC-E2E-REVIVAL-001 (Tier M, 26/26 gating AC PASS — 25 clean + 1 PASS-WITH-DEBT; e2e-revival deferred item 2/2). **(1) Detection + routing** (workflow skill pair, byte-identical local/template) — the `desktop-native` Detection Matrix row gains positive per-toolkit markers (AppKit `.xcodeproj`/`Package.swift`, WinUI/Win32 `.vcxproj`, Qt `CMakeLists.txt`/`.pro`, GTK meson/CMake), preserving most-specific-first ordering (Electron/Tauri rows stay checked first); the verbatim deferral sentence "There is no opt-in automation path for `desktop-native`" is removed while the genuine no-target graceful exit (pure libraries) is preserved; `--platform`/`--tool` flags, the Tool Matrix, and the Toolchain Probe table all gain desktop-native rows/tokens; the Phase 5 report-template platform enum gains `desktop-native`. **(2) Per-OS agent recipes** (`e2e-tester.md` pair) — the `### desktop-native` stub is replaced with three OS recipe subsections: macOS default `axcli` (cargo install, pinned version, AXUIElement tree snapshot) with appium-mac2-driver+WebdriverIO fallback and a TCC/Accessibility-permission blocker-report path (never a user prompt); Windows default FlaUI.WebDriver+WebdriverIO (W3C WebDriver2/UIA3, EXPERIMENTAL caveat, `/status` smoke probe) with pywinauto fallback; Linux default dogtail 2.x (AT-SPI2, `QT_LINUX_ACCESSIBILITY_ALWAYS_ON=1` for Qt, Wayland/ponytail caveat) with ydotool/xdotool-plus-screenshot-verification fallback; a documented universal last resort (AX-tree text snapshot loop, screenshot loop explicitly NOT CI-repeatable evidence) and the token-cost ordering doctrine (AX-tree text ≪ full tree JSON < screenshot < screenshot loop). WinAppDriver/appium-windows-driver are explicitly excluded (abandoned since 2020, zero references). Windows/Linux recipes are declarative documentation verified by structure/grep ACs; only the macOS path is locally probe-able and that probe is non-gating. **(3) Command surfaces** — the template `e2e.md.tmpl` and local `.claude/commands/moai/e2e.md` `argument-hint` gain the `desktop-native` `--platform` value; template command body stays at 6 non-empty LOC (well under the 20-LOC thin-command ceiling). **(4) Integrity** — no new agent file, no `catalog.yaml` entry/count/pin change (CI pins `expectedAgentCount=10`/`expectedTotal=38` untouched) aside from the mechanical `hash:`-line content-hash regeneration of the existing `e2e-tester` entry produced by the required `make build` (an explicit carve-out added to REQ-DNE-302 at run-phase, since `TestManifestHashFormat` enforces the regen); skill and agent pairs remain byte-identical across local/template trees (`diff` exit 0); the deferral-wording family regex (baseline: skill 3 + agent 1 matching lines per tree) returns 0 matches across all 4 files in both trees. `make build` and `go test ./internal/template/...` exit 0; `moai spec lint --strict` reports 0 errors. docs-site 4-locale documentation of the desktop-native lane is deferred to a future docs-site sync, matching the SPEC-DOCSITE-E2E-001 precedent. 🗿 MoAI

- **[SPEC-DOCSITE-E2E-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-DOCSITE-E2E-001/spec.md)** — docs-site (`adk.mo.ai.kr`) 4-locale documentation for the revived `/moai e2e` subcommand, deferred from SPEC-E2E-REVIVAL-001 (Tier M, 20/20 AC PASS). **(1) New 4-locale page** — `content/{en,ko,ja,zh}/utility-commands/moai-e2e.md`, authored ko-canonical then derived to en/ja/zh with identical 11-H2 structure and per-platform (web/mobile/desktop) toolchain tables. **(2) Navigation + menu registration** — `data/menu/main.yaml` sub-entry (4-locale name map) plus a `_meta.yaml` entry in all 4 locale content trees, ordered after `moai-feedback` at weight 90. **(3) Count-literal normalization** — the docs-site-wide stale "10 retained agents / 9 MoAI-custom / 10-agent catalog" literal family (English + ko/ja/zh locale forms, including a widened digit-boundary pattern catching spaced ja/zh phrasing) corrected to the 11/10 catalog across 41 files (40 markdown + `layouts/index.html` stat row), with `e2e-tester` inserted into six enumeration-class catalog surfaces (`advanced/agent-guide.md`, `claude-code/agentic/sub-agents.md`, `getting-started/introduction.md`, `getting-started/faq.md`, `advanced/claude-md-guide.md`, `core-concepts/what-is-moai-adk.md`) and left absent from five count-only surfaces where the literal update alone is correct. Hugo build stays warning-free (0 new WARN/ERROR vs. pre-flight baseline); the 16 pre-existing "no H1 heading" script-check errors on untouched files (decision-memory.md, harness-profiles.md, moai-goal.md, moai-harness.md × 4 locales) are unrelated to this change. `moai spec lint` reports 0 findings.

- **[SPEC-DOCSITE-ADVANCED-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-DOCSITE-ADVANCED-001/spec.md)** — docs-site v3.0 Advanced Guides content expansion (Tier L, 6 pages × 4-locale = 24 new markdown files + advanced/_meta.yaml parity debt fix + navigation registration). **6 new Advanced pages**: tokenomics-overview, token-budget, no-haiku-3tier, plan-type-profiles, self-evolving, autonomous-loops — authored in canonical KO and derived to EN/JA/ZH per oss-docs i18n rules. **M1 parity debt fix** (`49b3fda60`) — closed pre-existing advanced/_meta.yaml parity gap: 14 existing entries (catalog-system, decision-memory, harness-profiles, harness-v4-builder, hooks-reference, ultracode-workflows) now registered in all 4 locales (KO was missing 3, EN/JA/ZH were missing 6 each). **M5 navigation registration** (`e16aa5ee0`) — 6 new sub-entries added to data/menu/main.yaml Advanced section, per-locale _meta.yaml updated with 6 new entries each (24/24 parity verified). **Design regime verification**: 0 body-emoji violations (after token-budget inline-code emoji fix), 0 flowchart LR/RL matches (TD-only Mermaid), 0 blacklisted URLs (adk.mo.ai.kr only), H2 section-count parity 0 gaps across all 6 slugs × 4 locales, 132 icon shortcode invocations across 24 files. **Hugo build**: exit 0, 0 warnings, 24 new HTML pages generated (KO 126, EN/JA/ZH 124 each). **Known debt** (AC-DA-071 PASS-WITH-DEBT): sitemap includes section-level URL only (/ko/advanced/), not individual page URLs — pre-existing geekdoc sitemap configuration affecting ALL advanced pages equally (not specific to this SPEC's 6 new pages). 33/33 AC PASS (32 clean + 1 PASS-WITH-DEBT). 🗿 MoAI

- **[SPEC-DOCTOR-PROMOTION-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-DOCTOR-PROMOTION-001/spec.md)** — `moai doctor` gains a new "Plugin Deployment" check (`internal/cli/doctor.go` `checkPluginDeployment`, registered in the `moaiChecks` group) that detects a `plugin-deployed vX.Y.Z` marker in `.moai/config/sections/system.yaml` — recognizing both the `moai:`-rooted `version:` key shape and a top-level `version:` key shape — and reports a Warn with the deployed-vs-binary version comparison and a Detail-text-only promotion suggestion naming `moai init` as the path to the binary-managed template tree (4/4 AC PASS, Tier S). The check performs no automatic migration and no file writes (REQ-DP-002), returns OK with zero noise when no marker is present — plain semver or absent `system.yaml` (REQ-DP-003) — and degrades gracefully (OK/Warn with a parse note, never Fail) on an unreadable or malformed `system.yaml` (REQ-DP-004). Origin: REQ-BD-007, deferred from SPEC-MOC-BOOTSTRAP-DESKTOP-001 (sibling repo `claude.mo.ai.kr`).

- **[SPEC-E2E-REVIVAL-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-E2E-REVIVAL-001/spec.md)** — `/moai e2e` revived as a multi-platform, token-minimized E2E testing subsystem (Tier L, 28/28 AC PASS), superseding the web-only version retired by SPEC-SUBCOMMAND-RETIRE-001. **(1) Platform expansion** — web (Playwright CLI) + mobile (Maestro default, Appium/Detox alternatives) + desktop (Playwright-Electron for Electron apps, WebdriverIO + `@wdio/tauri-service` for Tauri apps), selected via project-type auto-detection (`web`/`mobile`/`desktop`/`mixed`/`desktop-native`/`none`); native-desktop (non-Electron/non-Tauri) automation is explicitly deferred to a follow-up SPEC. **(2) Dedicated specialist agent** — a new retained agent `e2e-tester` (`.claude/agents/moai/e2e-tester.md`, both template and local trees) drives detection, journey mapping, script creation, execution, and recording; it never prompts the user directly (structured `## Missing Inputs` blocker reports only) and all toolchain/journey selection routes through the orchestrator's `AskUserQuestion` channel. **(3) Token-minimization as a first-class requirement** — CLI-output-first execution (MCP only when the selected CLI cannot do the job), bounded in-context output (≤50 lines OR ≤2KB, file-redirect for verbose logs), native trace/recording facilities over MCP screenshot loops, and no MCP server as a hard dependency for any default platform path. **(4) Router + catalog reachability** — the `/moai` SKILL.md Priority 1 router and semantic (Priority 3) classification both regain an `e2e` entry; `CLAUDE.md` §3/§4 and the retained-agent catalog move from 10 to 11 agents (9→10 MoAI-custom); `internal/template/catalog.yaml` gains an `e2e-tester` entry; the Go tier-profile display surface (`model_policy.go`) gains a matching data row so the agent renders in the `moai web` model-policy preview. CI-guard count constants reconciled in the same milestones (`expectedAgentCount` 9→10, `expectedTotal` 37→38); `expectedSkillCount` unchanged at 28 (the workflow skill lives inside the existing `moai` skill directory). Template-First throughout: all three new artifacts (thin command, workflow skill, agent) authored in `internal/template/templates/` first, then synced to the local tree, byte-identical across both. Full suite (`go test ./...`), cross-platform build (darwin/windows amd64), `golangci-lint` (0 issues), and template-neutrality CI guards all green. docs-site 4-locale documentation for the revived subcommand is explicitly deferred to a follow-up SPEC.

- **[SPEC-CLI-TUX-V3-002](.moai/specs/SPEC-CLI-TUX-V3-002/spec.md)** — `moai init` redesign (CLI TUX modernization M2, Tier M, 18/18 AC PASS). **(1) Deferred self-update ordering** — the pre-wizard blocking `runBinaryUpdateStep` network call is removed; a CHECK-ONLY goroutine starts after wizard completion / first phase output and flushes a non-blocking stderr notice (with a `moai update` hint) under a bounded grace, never re-execs, never installs; existing skip conditions (`templates-only` flag, `EnvSkipBinaryUpdate`, dev-build detection) are preserved. **(2) huh v2 spike + unified wizard** — the huh v2.0.3 + bubbletea v2 pair spike confirmed the v1 YOffset scroll defect (`updateViewportHeight()` unconditional snap-to-top) is resolved by v2's `ensureVisible()` clamp-only scrolling; the wizard migrated to `charm.land/huh/v2`, consolidating unconditional questions into multi-field group pages while each conditional question keeps its own `WithHideFunc`-wrapped group, and the `wizardTotalSteps = 6` hardcoded stepper denominator is replaced by a single dynamic source (`TotalVisibleQuestions`, measured 5/8/9/12 across modes). **(3) Live progress feedback** — `internal/cli/printer`'s Spinner handle now animates via bubbles v2 `spinner.MiniDot` frame-cycling (gated on motion-allowed real TTY, goroutine-joined before the final line, `-race` clean), with a zero-ANSI/zero-animation plain fallback for non-TTY / `NO_COLOR` / `MOAI_REDUCED_MOTION`; `charm.land/bubbletea/v2` and `charm.land/bubbles/v2` land as direct go.mod dependencies (M1 residual-debt resolution), while huh v1.0.0 is retained for `update.go`/`profile_setup.go` (dual-major coexistence, M3 scope). **(4) Warning collector** — a `warnCollector` records every `Warn` and emits a single consolidated stderr summary panel exactly once at exit (zero panel when no warnings); stdout never carries warning text. **(5) Completion card + redirect hint** — the success completion card shows the `cd <project>` → `moai cc` → `/moai plan` next-action sequence with a warning-summary pointer when applicable, and re-running `moai init` against an already-initialized project without `--force` now surfaces a `moai update` redirect hint. **(6) Quality gate** — full suite (`go test ./... -count=1`) and `golangci-lint` (0 issues) green, `GOOS=windows`/`GOOS=linux` builds exit 0, and the `fmt.Printf`/`Println`/`Print(` ratchet in non-test `internal/cli` sources drops from the re-measured baseline 40 to 38 as two wizard call sites migrate to the unified form's Note-rendered stepper. 🗿 MoAI

- **[SPEC-WEBCONF-SIMPLIFY-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-WEBCONF-SIMPLIFY-001/spec.md)** — `moai web` configuration console simplified from 17 tabs to 6 surviving tabs (identity, language, launch, git_strategy, llm, agentfm). **(1) Tab consolidation** — the 11 removed tabs (`project`, `quality_extras`, `workflow`, `harness`, `ralph`/MoAI-Loop, `feedback`, `observability`, `security`, `mx`, `handoff`, `cache`) have their current values baked as template defaults in `internal/template/templates/.moai/config/sections/*.yaml` (ship to all users via `moai init`/`moai update`); the config keys persist for runtime consumption, only the web UI tab + write-route are removed (reclassified to `RouteExcluded`). **(2) 4-color sub-agent token-cost tier** — a net-new name-keyed lookup table (`internal/harness/v4manifest`) maps each of the 20 catalog agents to a display-only color tier (🔴 xhigh / 🟠 high / 🔵 medium / 🩵 low; distribution 🔴×4·🟠×4·🔵×5·🩵×7) INDEPENDENT of the agent's `effort` frontmatter (Option A: effort files UNTOUCHED, 0 `tier:` keys written); the agentfm UI renders the badge + tier-suggested (model, effort) defaults, individually overridable. **(3) Simplified surviving-tab surfaces** — git_strategy surfaces only `mode` + `merge_method` + `hooks.pre_push`; llm surfaces only the GLM tier mapping (`glm.models.{high,medium,low,fable}`); quality_extras is reduced to a single enable/disable toggle on the `launch` tab. **(4) Per-option description rendering** — a `FieldDef.Description` i18n-key field + a `fieldsets.templ` `fieldDescription` helper render a localized description below each field; per-option descriptions use the native `<option title>` via `data-i18n-title` (resolved by `app.js`). **(5) i18n 4-locale descriptions** — 24 `fieldDesc.<sectionID>.<fieldID>` keys × 4 locales (en/ko/ja/zh) = 96 strings; `TestI18nKeySetParity` passes with zero exemptions (REQ-WC-015/C-9). **(6) Template-default deltas** — 8 of 9 §D.Δ deliberate default-changes applied (workflow.execution_mode→auto, security.strict_mode→true, cache.enabled→true, git-strategy pre_push→enforce ×2, harness.effort_mapping→{low,medium,high}); the 9th (merge_method `squash`→`Squash`) was NOT applied — `Squash` is outside the `validMergeMethods` closed set (lowercase), so `merge_method` stays valid lowercase `squash` (documented debt). 24/24 AC PASS. Run-phase Route A main-direct, 9 milestones M1-M9 (`7b11d68bc`, `e0061ad34`, `cca120c70`, `5f97d32fe`, `0f13b4684`, `d0ea1c84e`, `7db2192db`) + run_commit_sha backfills. 🗿 MoAI

### Changed

- **[SPEC-GLM-EFFORT-TUNE-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-GLM-EFFORT-TUNE-001/spec.md)** — GLM effort overlay configuration tune-up: removed `builder-harness` from the coding-max override set (P1), added comments-only GLM reasoning-effort mapping exposure to both template and local `llm.yaml` (P2), and tightened overlay file comments to 3-state framing (P4) (Tier M, 18 AC: 16 PASS + 2 PASS-WITH-DEBT). **P1 Override set**: `glmCodingMaxOverrideAgents` is now singleton `{manager-develop}`; `builder-harness` removed from the override set and now reaches `reasoning-high` via the standard collapse (CG-mode cost-reduction alignment). **P2 Documentation exposure**: both `internal/template/templates/.moai/config/sections/llm.yaml` (template source) and `.moai/config/sections/llm.yaml` (local mirror) gained a comments-only mapping block describing the 3 reachable GLM reasoning states (`thinking-off` / `reasoning-high` / `reasoning-max`) and the collapse from Claude's 5-level effort vocabulary; NO runtime behavior change, NO new struct field (deferred to future user-override SPEC). **P4 Comment framing**: `glm_effort_overlay.go` comments corrected from the former "2-tier high/max" framing to the actual 3-state reality; docs-site grep found zero instances of the legacy 2-tier framing (M4 no-op). **Honesty caveat**: wire state is described as "implemented and wired; live validation of z.ai wire-effectiveness is pending" — NOT claimed as "validated" or "guaranteed" (verification-claim-integrity; carries forward MODEL-TIER-PLANTYPE-001 caveat). **Out-of-scope**: P3 (GLM wire-effectiveness live validation) is owned by SPEC-MODEL-TIER-PLANTYPE-001 follow-up #1.

- **[SPEC-HNS-PREFIX-RENAME-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-HNS-PREFIX-RENAME-001/spec.md)** — `/moai:harness` v4 Builder user-owned artifact prefix renamed from `harness-<name>` to lowercase `hns-<name>` (Tier M, 16/16 AC PASS). The rename spans four layers: **(1) Template Builder contract** — `harness-builder.md`, `harness-build-entry.md`, `moai/SKILL.md`, `moai-meta-harness/SKILL.md` (+ 5 secondary docs) now emit `hns-<name>-*` names for all six Builder artifact types. **(2) Go recognition logic** — `moai update` preservation, `moai harness list/edit/remove/doctor`, `moai doctor` skill-classification, frozen-guard, and prefix-conflict detection recognize `hns-*` as the new canonical user-owned prefix **in addition to** the two legacy generations (`harness-*`, `my-harness-*`) — tri-generation backward compatibility, verified end-to-end via `TestUpdateNamespaceHNS_TriGenerationPreservation` (7 artifacts across 3 generations survive `moai update` byte-identical, SHA-256 verified). **(3) CI guards** — the namespace-leak (`TestSplitHarnessNamespaceNoLeak`) and split-harness guards now cover both `harness-*` and `hns-*` patterns; a red-team probe (`hns-probe`) confirms the leak detector still fires on the new prefix. **(4) Local dev artifacts** — this repo's 3 dev-only specialists, 3 `harness-moaiadk-*` skills, and 1 Runner JS renamed to `hns-*` form with all cross-references updated. Directory structure is unchanged (`.claude/agents/harness/`, `.claude/commands/harness/`, `/harness:` command namespace keep their names — only artifact-name prefixes change). Zero test files deleted; full suite + cross-platform build (darwin/windows) green; template neutrality and language-neutrality guards pass with 0 leaked internal SPEC-ID references.
- **[SPEC-HARNESS-CADENCE-BUILD-001](.moai/specs/SPEC-HARNESS-CADENCE-BUILD-001/spec.md)** — `/moai:harness` v4 Builder gains cadence integration: build-time recurrence question, discovery-queue scheduling, ANALYZE-phase external research, and retroactive schedule attachment (Tier M, 35/36 AC PASS + 1 PASS-WITH-DEBT). **(1) Manifest schema** — additive-only `schedule` field (`interval`/`mechanism`/`mode`) on the harness manifest, validated in `internal/harness/v4manifest` (loop/cron fixtures, discovery-only mode enforcement, no silent mode default); zero behavior change for schedule-less manifests. **(2) Builder doctrine** — a recurrence question is asked at the PLAN→GENERATE gate round (`harness-builder.md`), and a new Recipe 4 ("Scheduled Harness Discovery") in `cadence-bridge.md` composes native `/loop` with read-only `/moai` discovery under the unchanged HARD invariant that scheduled runs never commit, never push, and never enter run-phase — findings land in a persisted `.moai/reports/cadence/<date>.md` queue for later review, not autonomous action. **(3) ANALYZE research sub-step** — the Builder's ANALYZE phase now checks official docs and Context7 before generating a harness, with MCP-unavailable fallback to WebFetch and a recorded skip rationale when omitted. **(4) Schedule Retrofit** — `harness-build-entry.md` detects scheduling intent on an existing harness command file and offers `moai harness edit` to attach a schedule after the fact, evaluated before normal re-entry. **(5) CLI schedule awareness** — `moai harness list` surfaces the declared schedule (text row + `--json` field, omitted when absent), `moai harness remove` unregisters cron/loop schedules before deletion, and `moai harness doctor` flags an invalid schedule mode as an ERROR finding. Template/live mirrors byte-identical across all 4 doctrine files; full suite (`go test ./...`, 99 packages) and `golangci-lint` (0 issues) green; cross-platform build (darwin/windows) verified. The 1 PASS-WITH-DEBT row is a pre-existing transient `StatusGitConsistency` lint warning unrelated to this change, self-resolving at sync close.

### Removed

- **Context7 MCP + gateguard advisory hook retirement** (`c745f4ea8`, doctrine cleanup, 191 files, +923/-4975) — the Context7 MCP dependency is retired across the entire doctrine/skill/rules/agent/docs surface: `mcp__context7__*` removed from every `allowed-tools` and the `settings.json` ask list, all "Context7 MCP" prose references replaced with the WebSearch/WebFetch fallback (the MoAI-native MCP-fallback strategy in `agent-common-protocol.md`), the `ddd-context7` and `context7-integration` modules dropped from `moai-workflow-testing`, and the MCP docs-site pages (`advanced/mcp-servers`, `claude-code/extensibility/mcp`; 4-locale) removed. The `gateguard-fact-force.sh` PreToolUse first-edit advisory hook (SPEC-HOOK-OFFICIAL-COMPLIANCE-001 / SPEC-OBSERVE-HYGIENE-001) is also retired — hook file + template mirror + `settings.json` registration deleted in the same sweep. Residual decimal-phase references in the docs-site command docs and `clean.md` cleaned up in the same commit. No SPEC (doctrine cleanup); run-phase Route A main-direct (Hybrid Trunk, 1-person OSS). docs-site 4-locale `settings-json.md` MCP-section consistency is tracked as a follow-up (auto-sync divergence). 🗿 MoAI

### Fixed

- **Profile display bug fix** (`f61073fe8`) — `internal/profile` GetCurrentName now consults launch.yaml ledger before defaulting to "default". The web UI previously showed "default" regardless of the active profile; now shows the actual profile name from the ledger. 🗿 MoAI

## [v3.0.0-rc11] - 2026-07-13
